**The ACTIVE nl "continue" loop (started 2026-08-27).** Fan the ~123 EN second-batch lcs-shell activities (games-build-walk output) out to **Dutch**, ONE per plan-mode session, in the SAME #1..#N order as the German fan-out. Rebuild-not-translate for the Dutch curriculum (SLO-kerndoelen, NOT Common Core). Build LOCAL only; **deploy once at the very end** of the whole nl program.

**Recipe per "continue":** operator says continue (in/into plan mode) → EnterPlanMode → find the next in order (child of the previous DE commit: `git rev-list --all --children | grep ^<prev-de-hash>` → the de child commit → `git show --stat` for the engine/id) → survey the EN source (`mini tools/<engine>-activit{y.js,ies.json}`, EN + de/it prose in `frontend/messages/activity-content/`) → **3-agent native Dutch ensemble** (general-purpose, NOT Explore): pedagoog (grade + strand + framing) + content creator (prose/title/intro/slug) + linguist (all in-activity strings). Pedagogue rules grade/strand FIRST (feeds title/slug/prose). → ExitPlanMode (the ONE approval gate) → build **activity-layer ONLY** (0 core/lcs-shell/Direction-A lines; `git diff --name-only` proves it) → full **§A.13.62 LOCAL DoD** → **commit + push, NO deploy, WITHOUT asking** ([[feedback_nl_fanout_autocommit_no_ask]]) → update this file → stop.

**The 5 activity-layer files (per activity):** `mini tools/<engine>-activity.js` (nl string blocks + any NUMW.nl number-words), `mini tools/<engine>-activities.json` (nl slug/page_title/page_intro + per-round promptL10n.nl if the engine uses it), `mini tools/<engine>-activity.html` (bump `<engine>-activity.js?v=N`), `frontend/messages/activity-content/nl.json` (Tier-1 prose override), `frontend/app/[locale]/activities/[slug]/page.tsx` (bump `ACTIVITY_WRAPPER_VERSION`; add per-activity GRADE_OVERRIDE/STRAND_OVERRIDE only when the pedagogue rules a divergence). Numbers/geometry LOCKED — only text localized.

**DoD gates (all must be green before commit):** `node --check` + JSON.parse; parity self-check (only nl keys added, geometry byte-identical, 0 core/shell); `verify-<engine>-core.js`; `verify-activity-content-nl.js`; `preflight-activity-routes.js`; `npx tsc --noEmit` (0 errors in the activities route — blog-test errors are pre-existing); `visual-qa-activity.js --activity=<id> --locale=nl` full sweep 320·360·412·768·1024·1366 × every round GREEN, then `--locale=en` regression, then restore nl; personal Read of 360/768/1024; visual-critic agent on the nl sweep; EN/Common-Core leak grep.

**Standing nl doctrine (recurring):**
- Framework name for nl users = **SLO-kerndoelen** (route chip); prose "sluit aan bij de SLO-kerndoelen". NEVER "Common Core"/CCSS code in nl-facing text (code stays the machine/SEO anchor only).
- Grade label map: grade_1→**Groep 3**, grade_2→**Groep 4**, grade_3→Groep 5 (groep runs +2 vs the CCSS grade number; K→Kleuters, PK→Peuters). **GRADE by SKILL, pedagogue-decisive** — genuine forks divergence is fine (nl #2 ruled Groep 4, a band above nl #1's Groep 3, matching pt/it).
- **Measurement (1.MD) strand auto-map "Meten en gegevens" is WRONG** (gegevens = data) → per-activity `STRAND_OVERRIDE nl: 'Meten en meetkunde'` for length/measurement activities. strand-names.ts NOT touched.
- de/het gender: diminutives (‑je/‑tje) are ALWAYS **het** → singular attributive adj bare ("Groot helpertje", "klein gaatje"), plural flips to ‑e ("de GROTE helpertjes"). Number "one" = **één** (accents, not "een"). Name possessive with apostrophe when it ends in a vowel sound ("Inchie's", "Tilly's"). "Tik op" (not Klik/Druk). Never goed/fout shaming. Address a class as "jullie", parent-facing prose as "je kind".
- Shell Check button renders **"Controleer"** in nl (match it in howToPlay prose).
- **Sprite-vs-noun consistency:** engines that don't vary the unit sprite by `unitKind` — name the unit by what's SHOWN, not the manifest kind (nl #2 lay-units: cube/clip rounds use "helpertjes", matching the worm sprite + the pt/it precedent, not blokjes/paperclips).
